1	use std::path::PathBuf;
2	
3	use clap::Args;
4	use jj_lib::object_id::ObjectId;
5	use jj_lib::repo::Repo;
6	
7	use log::{debug, info};
8	
9	use crate::common;
10	use crate::desc_helpers::{
11	    DEFAULT_ID_LEN, TitleMatch, VC_CONFIG_FILE, extract_bare_id, find_matching_commit,
12	    ochid_prefix_from_config, other_repo_from_config, validate_ochid,
13	};
14	use crate::toml_simple;
15	
16	#[derive(Args, Debug)]
17	pub struct ValidateDescArgs {
18	    /// Revision (with optional .. notation)
19	    #[arg(value_name = "REVISION")]
20	    pub pos_rev: Option<String>,
21	
22	    /// Number of commits (per dotted side)
23	    #[arg(value_name = "COMMITS")]
24	    pub pos_count: Option<usize>,
25	
26	    /// Revision(s) to scan
27	    #[arg(short, long, default_value = "@")]
28	    pub revision: String,
29	
30	    /// Number of commits to scan
31	    #[arg(short = 'n', long = "commits", value_name = "COMMITS")]
32	    pub limit: Option<usize>,
33	
34	    /// Path to jj repo
35	    #[arg(short = 'R', long, default_value = ".")]
36	    pub repo: PathBuf,
37	
38	    /// Path to the other repo [default: from .vc-config.toml]
39	    #[arg(long = "other-repo")]
40	    pub other_repo: Option<PathBuf>,
41	
42	    /// Expected changeID length
43	    #[arg(long = "id-len", default_value_t = DEFAULT_ID_LEN)]
44	    pub id_len: usize,
45	}
46	
47	/// Status of a single commit's ochid validation.
48	enum CommitStatus {
49	    Ok,
50	    Lost,                     // ochid marked as "lost" (unrecoverable)
51	    None_,                    // ochid marked as "none" (no counterpart)
52	    NeedsFixed(String),       // summary of issues
53	    MissingNoTitle,           // no ochid, no title to match on
54	    MissingNoMatch,           // no ochid, no title match in other repo
55	    MissingAmbiguous(usize),  // no ochid, multiple title matches
56	    MissingWithMatch(String), // no ochid, unique title match found
57	}
58	
59	pub fn validate_desc(args: &ValidateDescArgs) -> Result<(), Box<dyn std::error::Error>> {
60	    debug!("validate-desc: enter");
61	    let (workspace, repo) = common::load_repo(&args.repo)?;
62	
63	    // Resolve other repo: --other-repo flag, or fall back to .vc-config.toml
64	    let other_repo_path = if let Some(ref p) = args.other_repo {
65	        p.clone()
66	    } else {
67	        let config = toml_simple::toml_load(&args.repo.join(VC_CONFIG_FILE))?;
68	        args.repo.join(other_repo_from_config(&config)?)
69	    };
70	
71	    let (other_workspace, other_repo) = common::load_repo(&other_repo_path)?;
72	    let other_config = toml_simple::toml_load(&other_repo_path.join(VC_CONFIG_FILE))?;
73	    let other_prefix = ochid_prefix_from_config(&other_config)?;
74	
75	    let spec = common::resolve_spec(
76	        args.pos_rev.as_deref(),
77	        args.pos_count,
78	        &args.revision,
79	        args.limit,
80	        "@",
81	    );
82	
83	    let (ids, _anchor_index) = common::collect_ids(
84	        &workspace,
85	        &repo,
86	        &spec.rev,
87	        spec.desc_count,
88	        spec.anc_count,
89	    )?;
90	    if ids.is_empty() {
91	        return Err(format!("no commits found for revision '{}'", args.revision).into());
92	    }
93	
94	    let root_id = repo.store().root_commit_id().clone();
95	    let mut valid = 0;
96	    let mut lost = 0;
97	    let mut none = 0;
98	    let mut issues_count = 0;
99	    let mut missing = 0;
100	
101	    let col_header = "STAT CHANGEID      TITLE";
102	    info!("{col_header}");
103	
104	    for commit_id in &ids {
105	        if *commit_id == root_id {
106	            continue;
107	        }
108	        let commit = repo.store().get_commit(commit_id)?;
109	        let desc = commit.description();
110	        let change_hex = jj_lib::hex_util::encode_reverse_hex(commit.change_id().as_bytes());
111	        let change_short = &change_hex[..change_hex.len().min(12)];
112	        let first_line = desc.lines().next().unwrap_or(""); // OK: obvious
113	        let display_title = if first_line.is_empty() {
114	            "(no description set)"
115	        } else {
116	            first_line
117	        };
118	
119	        let current_ochid = common::extract_ochid(&commit);
120	
121	        let status = if let Some(ref ochid_val) = current_ochid {
122	            let bare = extract_bare_id(ochid_val);
123	            if bare == "lost" {
124	                CommitStatus::Lost
125	            } else if bare == "none" {
126	                CommitStatus::None_
127	            } else {
128	                let issues = validate_ochid(
129	                    ochid_val,
130	                    &other_prefix,
131	                    args.id_len,
132	                    &other_workspace,
133	                    &other_repo,
134	                );
135	                if issues.any() {
136	                    CommitStatus::NeedsFixed(issues.summary())
137	                } else {
138	                    CommitStatus::Ok
139	                }
140	            }
141	        } else {
142	            match find_matching_commit(&commit, &other_workspace, &other_repo)? {
143	                TitleMatch::NoTitle => CommitStatus::MissingNoTitle,
144	                TitleMatch::One(id) => {
145	                    let short_id = &id[..id.len().min(args.id_len)];
146	                    CommitStatus::MissingWithMatch(format!("{other_prefix}{short_id}"))
147	                }
148	                TitleMatch::Ambiguous(n) => CommitStatus::MissingAmbiguous(n),
149	                TitleMatch::None => CommitStatus::MissingNoMatch,
150	            }
151	        };
152	
153	        match status {
154	            CommitStatus::Ok => {
155	                valid += 1;
156	                info!("ok   {change_short}  {display_title}");
157	            }
158	            CommitStatus::Lost => {
159	                lost += 1;
160	                info!("lost {change_short}  {display_title}");
161	            }
162	            CommitStatus::None_ => {
163	                none += 1;
164	                info!("none {change_short}  {display_title}");
165	            }
166	            CommitStatus::NeedsFixed(summary) => {
167	                issues_count += 1;
168	                info!("err  {change_short}  {display_title}  [{summary}]");
169	            }
170	            CommitStatus::MissingWithMatch(ochid) => {
171	                missing += 1;
172	                info!("miss {change_short}  {display_title}  [match: {ochid}]");
173	            }
174	            CommitStatus::MissingNoTitle => {
175	                missing += 1;
176	                info!("miss {change_short}  {display_title}");
177	            }
178	            CommitStatus::MissingAmbiguous(n) => {
179	                missing += 1;
180	                info!("miss {change_short}  {display_title}  [{n} title matches, ambiguous]");
181	            }
182	            CommitStatus::MissingNoMatch => {
183	                missing += 1;
184	                info!("miss {change_short}  {display_title}  [no matching title in other repo]");
185	            }
186	        }
187	    }
188	
189	    let total = valid + lost + none + issues_count + missing;
190	    if total > 10 {
191	        info!("{col_header}");
192	    }
193	    info!("");
194	    info!(
195	        "{valid} valid, {lost} lost, {none} none, {issues_count} issues, {missing} missing (of {} total)",
196	        ids.len()
197	    );
198	    if issues_count > 0 {
199	        debug!("validate-desc: exit with issues");
200	        Err(format!("{issues_count} commit(s) have issues").into())
201	    } else {
202	        debug!("validate-desc: exit");
203	        Ok(())
204	    }
205	}
206

}

```
108	## Commit Message Style
109	
110	Use [Conventional Commits](https://www.conventionalcommits.org/) with
111	a version suffix:
112	
113	```
114	<type>: <short description> (<version>)
115	```
116	
117	- **Title**: target ~50 chars, short summary of *what* changed.
118	  Include the version. Common types: `feat`, `fix`, `refactor`,
119	  `test`, `docs`, `chore`.
120	- **App-repo body**: short intro paragraph (1–3 sentences), then a
121	  terse bullet list. Each bullet corresponds one-to-one with the
122	  edits structure already documented in `notes/chores-*.md` for
123	  this step — just the file and a one-line gist (e.g.
124	  `README.md: new Overview intro`). Do *not* restate the detail
125	  that lives in chores; the commit body is a scan-able index, not
126	  a duplicate. The chores section is the source of truth.
127	- **Session-repo body**: terse intro + a few session-activity
128	  bullets. Doesn't need to mirror chores since it describes
129	  in-session work, not code changes.
130	- Examples:
131	  - `feat: add fix-ochid subcommand (0.22.0)`
132	  - `fix: fix-ochid prefix bug (0.22.1)`
133	  - `refactor: deduplicate common CLI flags (0.21.1)`
134	
135	## Pre-commit Requirements
```
